# V1 · True Size: Version Infrastructure and World Scale Modes, Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Ship "V1 · True Size", the first student-facing version: a version label and frozen, versioned builds served at `wormsimulator.com/vN/`, plus Step 1b Part A, the World scale modes (Classic, Hybrid, True) with true-size leaves and props, solid trunks, rocks and log, and a camera that stays out of them.

**Architecture:** One table (`src/surface/worldScale.ts`) holds a `ScaleSpec` per mode: size multipliers and that mode's tree, rock, log, clutter and distant-ring layout. Every size and placement module reads the active spec (`scaleSpec()`); caches keyed on the spec object refresh when `setScale()` changes it. Solid footprints (`src/surface/obstacles.ts`) are built from the same layout and used by the worm (slide along), the hero leaves (unwedge, respawn) and the camera (sight line). Switching mode rebuilds only the "forest" group (`world.rebuildForest()`), compiles the new materials while the old ones are still alive (so three.js reuses every program: zero shader compiles, checked in e2e), then frees the old forest. The disturbance map is sized once for True's biggest leaves; each mode stamps only as far as its own leaves need. Version infrastructure: `src/version.ts` → Start label and a `<meta name="wormsim-version">` tag; `npm run release -- N` freezes `dist/index.html` into `releases/vN/`; Render's build copies `releases/` into the site.

**Tech Stack:** Vite 8, TypeScript 7 (strict), three 0.186 (WebGL 2), vite-plugin-singlefile, Vitest 5 (unit), Playwright (e2e, SwiftShader), Node 24 scripts (`.mjs`).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-03-step1b-world-scale-and-first-bite-design.md`, section 4 (Part A). Part B (section 5, Level 1) is out of scope. The only Part B item that section 4 mentions, keeping props clear of Level 1's zones (4.4), is **deferred to Part B**: Level 1 does not exist yet, so there are no zones to keep clear. Part B's plan must add that test.

**Co-development:** `CLAUDE.md` and `docs/codev/README.md`. V1's name is fixed: "V1 · True Size".

---

## Before you start

- **Branch:** work on `step1b` (it already holds Part 0, the leaf jitter fix). The untracked `.claude/` folder is not part of this work: always `git add` the exact files listed, never `git add -A`.
- **Commands** (from the repo root):
  - unit tests: `npx vitest run` (one file: `npx vitest run tests/unit/<name>.test.ts`; one test: add `-t "<part of its name>"`)
  - types: `npx tsc --noEmit`
  - build and single-file check: `npm run verify:build`
  - browser tests: `npm run test:e2e` (builds first; one file: `npm run build && npx playwright test tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts`)
- **Before Task 1** `npx vitest run` reports **55 files, 529 tests passed**, and `npm run test:e2e` **22 passed**. Each task states the new totals. Every task leaves `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx vitest run`, `npm run verify:build` and `npm run test:e2e` green.
- **Every commit message ends with** a blank line and `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>` (the commit commands below include it).
- **Conventions:** named exports only; unit tests import from `'../../src/...'`; no allocation in per-step or per-frame code you add (preallocated objects and typed arrays); no shader recompiles on a preset or scale change; GLSL with a TS twin stays in step (`tests/e2e/glslParity.spec.ts`).
- **Rendering tasks** are marked **(rendering)**. Reviewers of those tasks read `docs/superpowers/visual-quality-brief.md` first and look at the screenshots the task asks for.
- **Units:** the worm is 3.5 units long and stands for a 15 cm earthworm, so 1 cm ≈ 0.23 units. The world stays 40 × 40 units.
- **Global scale state in tests.** `setScale()` changes module state that many modules read. Vitest isolates test files, so a file that switches modes resets with `afterAll(() => setScale(DEFAULT_SCALE))`. Until Task 15 `DEFAULT_SCALE` is `'classic'`; Task 15 makes it `'true'` and pins the Classic-specific test files.
- **Scratch screenshots.** Rendering tasks ask for screenshots with a throwaway script. Create it once (it is not committed: `.cache/` is git-ignored):

```bash
mkdir -p .cache/shots && cat > .cache/shot.mjs <<'SHOT'
// node .cache/shot.mjs <out.png> [query] [actions...]   actions: click:Name | radio:Name | key:Code | wait:ms | hold:Code:ms
import { chromium } from '@playwright/test';
import path from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
const [out, query = '', ...actions] = process.argv.slice(2);
const browser = await chromium.launch({ args: ['--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--use-angle=swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'] });
const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1366, height: 768 } });
const errors = [];
page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(e.message));
page.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error') errors.push(m.text()); });
await page.goto(pathToFileURL(path.resolve('dist/index.html')).href + query);
await page.waitForTimeout(1500);
for (const a of actions) {
  const [k, v, ms] = a.split(':');
  if (k === 'click') await page.getByRole('button', { name: v, exact: true }).click();
  else if (k === 'radio') await page.getByRole('radio', { name: new RegExp('^' + v) }).check();
  else if (k === 'key') await page.keyboard.press(v);
  else if (k === 'wait') await page.waitForTimeout(Number(v));
  else if (k === 'hold') { await page.keyboard.down(v); await page.waitForTimeout(Number(ms)); await page.keyboard.up(v); }
}
await page.screenshot({ path: out });
console.log('errors:', errors.length ? errors.join(' | ') : 'none');
await browser.close();
SHOT
```

  Run it after `npm run build`, for example `node .cache/shot.mjs .cache/shots/true.png "?scale=true&preset=high" click:Crawl hold:ArrowUp:3000`, then open the PNG and look at it.

## Decisions made while planning (verified in a scratch build)

Every code block in this plan was built and tested in a scratch worktree in this task order; each task's commit left unit tests, e2e tests and `npm run verify:build` green. These choices fill gaps in the spec or deviate from it; Task 18 records them in the spec.

| Topic | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Release check | The build carries `<meta name="wormsim-version" content="V1 · True Size">`; `npm run release` reads it | A robust check that does not depend on how the minifier writes "·" in the script |
| `/v1` and `/v1/` | No redirect route | Render serves `v1/index.html` at both, and never applies a route where a file exists (Render docs, "Redirects and rewrites"; feedback.render.com "Configurable trailing slashes"). The single file has no relative links, so both URLs work |
| Saved settings | A write keeps fields this build does not know; `updateSettings()` patches | An older version (V1) must not wipe a later version's fields; the Graphics menu and World scale panel must not overwrite each other |
| Layouts | Classic is byte-for-byte Step 1a (checksum tests). Hybrid: 5 seeded trees (seed 15), 5 rocks, the log at (9, 9). True: 2 seeded corner trees (seed 284), 5 rocks, a 40-unit log across the view at z = 15.5 that runs off both edges | Every solid footprint stays off the speed test's loop, the spawn and (Hybrid, True) the straight run; tests check each mode |
| Footprints | Measured from the drawn meshes: trunk root flare (birch 0.52, maple 1.08, a birch clump 0.95, times the trunk's size), rocks 1.2 × size, the log 1.15 × radius plus 0.18 at its broken ends | Step 1a's hero-leaf footprints were smaller than the drawn props; the worm would have sunk into flares and rock flanks |
| Trees | Height, girth, lean, limbs, curls and crowns all scale; the bark pattern scales with the trunk (`aBark.y / grow`) | Unscaled bark turned a True birch log into a picket fence |
| Disturbance map | 96 × 96 cells over 12 m (12.5 cm cells as before), reach 3.65, on-top reach kept at 0.2 m. Each mode stamps only out to its biggest leaf plus the drape's foot (Classic 1.6 m, exactly Step 1a's reach) | Sized once for True (biggest leaf radius 3.06); Classic's stamping work is unchanged |
| Big leaves | A leaf with radius over 1.3 lifts level (a lid) instead of tilting onto the body; only True's leaves are that big | A tilted plane clipped a worm curving under a 5 m leaf (9 of 10,000+ checks, by up to 5 mm) |
| Leaf sizes | Litter, detail and hero leaves are all multiplied by the species' True factor: the litter is real length on average; detail and hero leaves keep their Classic share of it (a third and about a half) | Keeps the detail layer dense near the worm. Counts scale with 1 / silhouette area (True: 395 litter, 237 to 422 detail, 3 to 8 hero leaves) |
| Big hero leaves | Above 1 m they tilt and settle at rates divided by length cubed | Keeps True within Part 0's jitter thresholds (measured 2.8% and 5.3 mm against 3% and 6 mm) |
| Near clutter (Ultra) | Not scaled | Its shader is bounded by NEAR_CLUTTER_REACH; scaled items would shrink away near the worm. They read as young sedge and mushroom buttons |
| Camera | A fan of three sight lines (±0.5 rad), stops 0.35 short, eases in at 5/s and out at 2.5/s, never nearer than 0.15 | Measured at most 0.14 m per step and smooth (jerk ≤ 0.05) against 0.08 for a hard turn alone |
| Switching | A pick is built 400 ms after the last pick (arrowing from Classic to True builds once); leaving the screen builds a waiting pick at once | Each build takes a noticeable moment on a Chromebook |
| Start screen | Crawl, World scale, Graphics, Speed test | Play and Free crawl arrive with Part B |

### Task 1: Version module and the Start-screen label

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/ui/menus.ts`
- Modify: `src/ui/parchment.css`
- Create: `src/version.ts`
- Create: `tests/e2e/version.spec.ts`
- Create: `tests/unit/version.test.ts`
- Modify: `vite.config.ts`

Every build shows its version (CLAUDE.md). `src/version.ts` holds the number and name; the Start card shows "V1 · True Size" as a small right-aligned line under the menu (18 px, bold, the soft ink `--ink-soft` on parchment: well over 7:1). It is plain text, so it takes no focus and the menu's focus order is unchanged. The build also stamps a meta tag that Task 2's release script checks. `vite.config.ts` imports `./src/version.js` (the `.js` spelling resolves to the `.ts` file; without an extension Vite's config loader prints a warning).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/e2e/version.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import path from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { VERSION_LABEL } from '../../src/version';

const PAGE = pathToFileURL(path.resolve('dist/index.html')).href;

/** WCAG relative luminance of an `rgb(r, g, b)` colour. */
function luminance(rgb: string): number {
  const [r, g, b] = (rgb.match(/\d+(\.\d+)?/g) ?? []).slice(0, 3).map((v) => {
    const c = Number(v) / 255;
    return c <= 0.03928 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
  });
  return 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b;
}

test('the Start screen shows the version label, at least 18 px and 7:1 on the parchment', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto(PAGE);
  const label = page.getByText(VERSION_LABEL, { exact: true });
  await expect(label).toBeVisible();
  const style = await label.evaluate((e) => {
    const s = getComputedStyle(e);
    return { size: parseFloat(s.fontSize), color: s.color };
  });
  expect(style.size).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(18);
  // The card's flat parchment (--parchment, #f8eed6) under the label.
  const L1 = luminance(style.color);
  const L2 = luminance('rgb(248, 238, 214)');
  expect((Math.max(L1, L2) + 0.05) / (Math.min(L1, L2) + 0.05)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(7);
  // Decorative to keyboard users: it takes no focus, so the menu's focus order is unchanged.
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Crawl', exact: true })).toBeFocused();
});

test('the built page carries the version meta tag', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto(PAGE);
  await expect(page.locator('meta[name="wormsim-version"]')).toHaveAttribute('content', VERSION_LABEL);
});
```

Create `tests/unit/version.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { VERSION, VERSION_LABEL, VERSION_META } from '../../src/version';

describe('version', () => {
  it('is V1 · True Size', () => {
    expect(VERSION).toEqual({ number: 1, name: 'True Size' });
    expect(VERSION_LABEL).toBe('V1 · True Size');
  });

  it('has a whole number from 1 and a 2 to 4 word name (docs/codev/README.md)', () => {
    expect(Number.isInteger(VERSION.number) && VERSION.number >= 1).toBe(true);
    const words = VERSION.name.trim().split(/\s+/);
    expect(words.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    expect(words.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(4);
  });

  it('stamps the build with a meta tag the release script reads', () => {
    expect(VERSION_META).toBe('<meta name="wormsim-version" content="V1 · True Size">');
  });
});
```

Create `src/version.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The version students play (docs/codev/README.md): a number and a short name, set when the
 * version is planned and never changed after release. Shown on the Start screen and stamped into
 * the built page as VERSION_META, which `npm run release` checks before it freezes a snapshot.
 * Bump both for the next version's first commit.
 */
export const VERSION = { number: 1, name: 'True Size' } as const;

/** "V1 · True Size": the Start-screen label, slides, form, Canvas post and changelog all use it. */
export const VERSION_LABEL = `V${VERSION.number} · ${VERSION.name}`;

/** Injected into the built page's <head> (vite.config.ts). scripts/releaseLib.mjs parses it. */
export const VERSION_META = `<meta name="wormsim-version" content="${VERSION_LABEL}">`;
```

### Task 2: Release script: freeze a build as releases/vN

**Files:**
- Modify: `package.json`
- Create: `scripts/release.mjs`
- Create: `scripts/releaseLib.d.mts`
- Create: `scripts/releaseLib.mjs`
- Create: `tests/unit/release.test.ts`

`npm run release -- <N>` refuses to overwrite an existing `releases/vN/` (released versions are frozen) unless `--emergency-fix` is given, builds with the single-file check, refuses a build whose meta label is not VN, then copies `dist/index.html` to `releases/vN/index.html`. It prints the git command; it does not commit. The pure rules live in `scripts/releaseLib.mjs` so Vitest can test them; `releaseLib.d.mts` gives TypeScript its types (the tests are type-checked by `tsc`).

**Do not cut `releases/v1/` in this plan.** Matt tests the root build first; cutting V1 is a release step after his approval.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/unit/release.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  parseReleaseArgs, parseVersionLabel, checkTarget, checkBuild, releasePath,
} from '../../scripts/releaseLib.mjs';
import { VERSION, VERSION_META } from '../../src/version';

const page = (meta: string) => `<!doctype html><html><head>\n    ${meta}\n</head><body></body></html>`;

describe('parseReleaseArgs', () => {
  it('reads the version number and the emergency flag', () => {
    expect(parseReleaseArgs(['1'])).toEqual({ version: 1, emergencyFix: false });
    expect(parseReleaseArgs(['12', '--emergency-fix'])).toEqual({ version: 12, emergencyFix: true });
    expect(parseReleaseArgs(['--emergency-fix', '3'])).toEqual({ version: 3, emergencyFix: true });
  });
  it('rejects a missing, non-whole, zero or extra argument with the usage line', () => {
    for (const bad of [[], ['v1'], ['1.5'], ['0'], ['-2'], ['1', '2'], ['1', '--force']]) {
      expect(() => parseReleaseArgs(bad)).toThrow(/Usage: npm run release -- <N>/);
    }
  });
});

describe('parseVersionLabel', () => {
  it('reads back what the build stamps (src/version.ts)', () => {
    expect(parseVersionLabel(page(VERSION_META))).toEqual({ number: VERSION.number, name: VERSION.name });
  });
  it('is null without the tag, or with a malformed one', () => {
    expect(parseVersionLabel(page(''))).toBeNull();
    expect(parseVersionLabel(page('<meta name="wormsim-version" content="1 True Size">'))).toBeNull();
    expect(parseVersionLabel(page('<meta name="wormsim-version" content="V0 · Zero">'))).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('checkTarget: released versions are frozen', () => {
  it('allows a new version', () => {
    expect(checkTarget(2, false, false)).toBeNull();
  });
  it('refuses to overwrite an existing release', () => {
    expect(checkTarget(1, true, false)).toMatch(/releases\/v1\/ already exists.*frozen.*--emergency-fix/s);
  });
  it('overwrites only with --emergency-fix', () => {
    expect(checkTarget(1, true, true)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('checkBuild', () => {
  it('accepts a build labelled with the version being released', () => {
    expect(checkBuild(page('<meta name="wormsim-version" content="V3 · Racing the Frost">'), 3)).toBeNull();
  });
  it('refuses a build labelled with another version, or none', () => {
    expect(checkBuild(page('<meta name="wormsim-version" content="V2 · First Bite">'), 3))
      .toMatch(/labelled V2 · First Bite, not V3.*src\/version\.ts/s);
    expect(checkBuild(page(''), 3)).toMatch(/no version label/);
  });
});

describe('releasePath', () => {
  it('is releases/vN/index.html', () => {
    expect(releasePath(4)).toBe('releases/v4/index.html');
  });
});
```

Create `scripts/release.mjs`:

```js
// Freezes the current build as version N (docs/codev/README.md, "Frozen builds"):
//   npm run release -- <N> [--emergency-fix]
// 1. Refuses if releases/vN/ already exists (released versions are frozen) unless --emergency-fix.
// 2. Builds and runs the single-file check (npm run verify:build).
// 3. Refuses if the build is not labelled VN (src/version.ts).
// 4. Copies dist/index.html to releases/vN/index.html. Commit it yourself; Render serves it at /vN/.
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { copyFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { checkBuild, checkTarget, parseReleaseArgs, releasePath } from './releaseLib.mjs';

function fail(message) {
  console.error(`release: ${message}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

let args;
try {
  args = parseReleaseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
} catch (e) {
  fail(e.message);
}
const { version, emergencyFix } = args;
const target = releasePath(version);

const refused = checkTarget(version, existsSync(path.dirname(target)), emergencyFix);
if (refused) fail(refused);

execFileSync('npm', ['run', 'verify:build'], { stdio: 'inherit' });
const wrong = checkBuild(readFileSync('dist/index.html', 'utf8'), version);
if (wrong) fail(wrong);

mkdirSync(path.dirname(target), { recursive: true });
copyFileSync('dist/index.html', target);
console.log(`\nrelease: wrote ${target}${emergencyFix ? ' (emergency fix)' : ''}.`);
console.log(`Next: git add ${target} && git commit -m "release: V${version}" && git push`);
console.log(`Then check https://wormsimulator.com/v${version}/ serves it.`);
```

Create `scripts/releaseLib.d.mts`:

```ts
export interface ReleaseArgs { version: number; emergencyFix: boolean }
export function parseReleaseArgs(argv: readonly string[]): ReleaseArgs;
export function parseVersionLabel(html: string): { number: number; name: string } | null;
export function releasePath(n: number): string;
export function checkTarget(n: number, exists: boolean, emergencyFix: boolean): string | null;
export function checkBuild(html: string, n: number): string | null;
```

Create `scripts/releaseLib.mjs`:

```js
// The pure rules behind `npm run release -- <N>` (scripts/release.mjs), kept apart so they are
// unit-tested (tests/unit/release.test.ts). Types: releaseLib.d.mts.

const USAGE = 'Usage: npm run release -- <N> [--emergency-fix]   (N is the version number, e.g. 1)';

/** The command-line arguments: a whole version number from 1, and the optional emergency flag. */
export function parseReleaseArgs(argv) {
  const emergencyFix = argv.includes('--emergency-fix');
  const rest = argv.filter((a) => a !== '--emergency-fix');
  if (rest.length !== 1 || !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(rest[0])) throw new Error(USAGE);
  return { version: Number(rest[0]), emergencyFix };
}

/** The version a built page is stamped with (src/version.ts VERSION_META), or null. */
export function parseVersionLabel(html) {
  const m = html.match(/<meta name="wormsim-version" content="V([1-9]\d*) · ([^"<>]+)">/);
  return m ? { number: Number(m[1]), name: m[2] } : null;
}

/** Where version n's frozen snapshot lives (served at /vN/). */
export function releasePath(n) {
  return `releases/v${n}/index.html`;
}

/** Why version n cannot be written (its folder `exists`), or null when it can. */
export function checkTarget(n, exists, emergencyFix) {
  if (!exists || emergencyFix) return null;
  return `releases/v${n}/ already exists. Released versions are frozen: students and slides point at it. `
    + 'For a real emergency fix, run again with --emergency-fix and note the fix in the version doc '
    + 'and its CHANGELOG entry.';
}

/** Why the built page (`html`) is not version n, or null when it is. */
export function checkBuild(html, n) {
  const v = parseVersionLabel(html);
  if (!v) return 'The build has no version label (meta wormsim-version). Is vite.config.ts stamping it?';
  if (v.number !== n) {
    return `The build is labelled V${v.number} · ${v.name}, not V${n}. Set VERSION in src/version.ts first.`;
  }
  return null;
}
```

### Task 3: Serve frozen releases at /vN/ on Render

**Files:**
- Modify: `docs/hosting.md`
- Modify: `render.yaml`
- Create: `scripts/copy-releases.mjs`
- Modify: `scripts/releaseLib.d.mts`
- Modify: `scripts/releaseLib.mjs`
- Create: `tests/e2e/releases.spec.ts`
- Modify: `tests/unit/release.test.ts`

Render's build gains `node scripts/copy-releases.mjs`, which copies every `releases/vN/index.html` into `dist/vN/`. No `releases/` folder (today) or an empty one is fine. Render serves `dist/v1/index.html` at both `/v1/` and `/v1`, and never applies a redirect where a file exists, so no route is added; the existing headers apply to every path. The new e2e test copies a stand-in v1 (today's build) plus any real releases into a scratch site with the real script and checks each one runs offline with zero network requests, as the root build does. Once V1 is cut, the frozen file is checked on every e2e run.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/e2e/releases.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { copyFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readdirSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { VERSION_LABEL } from '../../src/version';

/**
 * Render serves every frozen version at /vN/ by copying releases/ into dist/ after the build
 * (scripts/copy-releases.mjs, render.yaml). This runs that copy into a scratch folder, with a
 * stand-in v1 made from today's build plus every real release in the repo, and checks each copy
 * runs offline from its folder exactly as the root build does.
 */
let site = '';
let versions: string[] = [];

test.beforeAll(() => {
  const root = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'wormsim-releases-'));
  const from = path.join(root, 'releases');
  site = path.join(root, 'dist');
  mkdirSync(path.join(from, 'v1'), { recursive: true });
  mkdirSync(site, { recursive: true });
  copyFileSync('dist/index.html', path.join(site, 'index.html'));
  copyFileSync('dist/index.html', path.join(from, 'v1', 'index.html'));
  // Real releases (once cut) are checked too; a real v1 replaces the stand-in.
  if (existsSync('releases')) {
    for (const v of readdirSync('releases').filter((n) => /^v[1-9]\d*$/.test(n))) {
      mkdirSync(path.join(from, v), { recursive: true });
      copyFileSync(path.join('releases', v, 'index.html'), path.join(from, v, 'index.html'));
    }
  }
  execFileSync('node', ['scripts/copy-releases.mjs', '--from', from, '--to', site]);
  versions = readdirSync(from);
  test.info().annotations.push({ type: 'releases', description: versions.join(', ') });
});

test.afterAll(() => {
  if (site) rmSync(path.dirname(site), { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('every release copied into the site runs offline with zero network requests', async ({ page, context }) => {
  expect(versions).toContain('v1');
  await context.setOffline(true);
  for (const v of versions) {
    const outside: string[] = [];
    const errors: string[] = [];
    const onRequest = (r: { url(): string }) => { if (!/^(file|data|blob):/.test(r.url())) outside.push(r.url()); };
    page.on('request', onRequest);
    page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(e.message));
    await page.goto(pathToFileURL(path.join(site, v, 'index.html')).href);
    const crawl = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Crawl', exact: true });
    await expect(crawl).toBeFocused();
    await crawl.click();
    await page.keyboard.down('ArrowUp');
    await page.waitForTimeout(500);
    await page.keyboard.up('ArrowUp');
    expect(outside, v).toEqual([]);
    expect(errors, v).toEqual([]);
    page.off('request', onRequest);
    page.removeAllListeners('pageerror');
  }
});

test('the stand-in v1 copy carries this build\'s version label', async ({ page }) => {
  test.skip(existsSync('releases/v1'), 'a real v1 is frozen: its label is its own');
  await page.goto(pathToFileURL(path.join(site, 'v1', 'index.html')).href);
  await expect(page.locator('meta[name="wormsim-version"]')).toHaveAttribute('content', VERSION_LABEL);
});
```

```markdown
## Updating the game

Every push to `main` on GitHub rebuilds and redeploys automatically. The root,
```

```markdown
Students only ever get a version link such as `https://wormsimulator.com/v1/`
(docs/codev/README.md). Each one is a frozen copy of the single-file build:

1. When Matt has checked the root build, freeze it: `npm run release -- 1` (the version number).
   It builds, checks that the Start-screen label says V1, and copies `dist/index.html` to
   `releases/v1/index.html`.

   into the site, so the snapshot is served at `/v1/` and `/v1`, next to the root build.
3. Check `https://wormsimulator.com/v1/` loads and shows "V1 · …" on the Start screen.

The first time, check the site's **Settings → Build Command** in the Render Dashboard reads
`npm ci && npm run build && node scripts/copy-releases.mjs`. A Blueprint with auto-sync picks up the
`render.yaml` change on its own; if it still shows the old command, paste the new one in.

A released version is never rebuilt. `npm run release` refuses to overwrite `releases/vN/` unless
you add `--emergency-fix`, and an emergency fix is noted in that version's doc and changelog entry.
Every version runs on the same site, so they share saved settings (see CLAUDE.md, "One origin,
shared saves").

## Before using it with a class
```

```yaml
# Render Blueprint: builds the game into one static file and serves it at wormsimulator.com.
# Students only ever load wormsimulator.com (Render's CDN); GitHub is only where Render pulls the code from.
# The root (/) is the build of main, Matt's preview. Each frozen version in releases/vN/ is copied
# into dist/ after the build and served at /vN/ (and /vN: Render serves a folder's index.html at
# both, and a route never overrides a file that exists, so no redirect is needed).
services:
  - type: web
    name: wormsimulator
    runtime: static
    branch: main
    buildCommand: npm ci && npm run build && node scripts/copy-releases.mjs
    staticPublishPath: ./dist
    domains:
      # Render adds www.wormsimulator.com automatically and redirects it here.
```

Create `scripts/copy-releases.mjs`:

```js
// Copies every frozen version (releases/vN/index.html) into the build, so Render serves it at
// /vN/ next to the root build. Runs after `npm run build` on Render (render.yaml).
//   node scripts/copy-releases.mjs [--from releases] [--to dist]
// No releases yet (no folder, or no vN folders in it) is fine: nothing is copied.
import { copyFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { releasesToServe } from './releaseLib.mjs';

const arg = (name, fallback) => {
  const i = process.argv.indexOf(name);
  return i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1] ? process.argv[i + 1] : fallback;
};
const from = arg('--from', 'releases');
const to = arg('--to', 'dist');

if (!existsSync(path.join(to, 'index.html'))) {
  console.error(`copy-releases: ${to}/index.html is missing. Run npm run build first.`);
  process.exit(1);
}
const versions = existsSync(from) ? releasesToServe(readdirSync(from)) : [];
let copied = 0;
for (const v of versions) {
  const src = path.join(from, v, 'index.html');
  if (!existsSync(src)) {
    console.error(`copy-releases: ${src} is missing (every release folder holds one index.html).`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  mkdirSync(path.join(to, v), { recursive: true });
  copyFileSync(src, path.join(to, v, 'index.html'));
  copied++;
}
console.log(copied ? `copy-releases: served ${versions.join(', ')}` : 'copy-releases: no releases yet');
```

In `scripts/releaseLib.d.mts`:

Add at the end of the file:

```ts
export function releasesToServe(names: readonly string[]): string[];
```

In `scripts/releaseLib.mjs`:

Add at the end of the file:

```js
/** The release folders to serve (`v1`, `v2`, ...) among a folder's entry names, in version order. */
export function releasesToServe(names) {
  return names.filter((n) => /^v[1-9]\d*$/.test(n)).sort((a, b) => Number(a.slice(1)) - Number(b.slice(1)));
}
```

### Task 4: The ScaleSpec table and leaf counts by area

**Files:**
- Create: `src/surface/leafScale.ts`
- Create: `src/surface/worldScale.ts`
- Create: `tests/unit/worldScale.test.ts`

One `ScaleSpec` per mode (spec 4.1, 4.2). Classic is exactly Step 1a's numbers. Hybrid and True carry their own layouts (found by search in the scratch build and checked by Task 6's tests): trees as `{ count, seed, clear }`, explicit rock lists at Classic size (drawn `rock` times bigger), the log's Classic radius and length (scaled by `log`), clutter count and seed, and the distant ring's size and count. True leaf multipliers make each species' average litter leaf its real length (aspen 5.5, ash leaflet 8, sugar maple 11, basswood 12, red oak 15 cm): the multiplier is the real length in units over `LEAF_SIZE[kind] × 0.9`, the Classic average. `DEFAULT_SCALE` stays `'classic'` until Task 15, so nothing changes for players yet.

`leafCountScale` scales a layer's count by 1 / leaf area, summing each species' silhouette area (randomly strewn leaves cover a share of the floor that depends only on their total area). For True it is 0.264: 1,500 litter leaves become 395.

Create `tests/unit/worldScale.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import {
  SCALE_SPECS, SCALE_MODES, SCALE_LABELS, SCALE_DESCRIPTIONS, DEFAULT_SCALE, REAL_LEAF_CM, UNITS_PER_CM,
  isScaleMode, scaleSpec, setScale,
} from '../../src/surface/worldScale';
import { LEAF_KINDS } from '../../src/surface/leafLitter';
import { LEAF_SIZE } from '../../src/surface/leafPalette';
import { leafCountScale, scaledLeafCount } from '../../src/surface/leafScale';

afterEach(() => { setScale(DEFAULT_SCALE); });

describe('world scale table', () => {
  it('has the three modes, each with a label and a one-line description', () => {
    expect(SCALE_MODES).toEqual(['classic', 'hybrid', 'true']);
    for (const m of SCALE_MODES) {
      expect(SCALE_SPECS[m].mode).toBe(m);
      expect(SCALE_LABELS[m].length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(SCALE_DESCRIPTIONS[m]).toMatch(/\.$/);
    }
    expect(SCALE_DESCRIPTIONS).toEqual({
      classic: 'The forest as it is now.',
      hybrid: 'Small things real size, big things half.',
      true: 'Everything real size, next to a real worm.',
    });
  });

  it('Classic is exactly the Step 1a world', () => {
    const c = SCALE_SPECS.classic;
    expect(c).toMatchObject({ mushroom: 1, fern: 1, sedge: 1, rock: 1, log: 1, birch: 1, maple: 1, clutter: 64, clutterSeed: 5 });
    expect(c.trees).toEqual({ count: 14, seed: 7, clear: 4 });
    expect(c.logSite).toEqual({ x: 3.9, z: 4.7, yaw: -0.5, length: 4.4, radius: 0.32 });
    expect(c.rocks).toHaveLength(7);
    for (const k of LEAF_KINDS) expect(c.leaf[k]).toBe(1);
    expect([c.distantScale, c.distantCount]).toEqual([1, 72]);
  });

  it('matches the spec table (section 4.1)', () => {
    const h = SCALE_SPECS.hybrid;
    const t = SCALE_SPECS.true;
    expect(h).toMatchObject({ mushroom: 3, rock: 4, fern: 7, sedge: 7, log: 4.5, birch: 4.5, maple: 4.5 });
    expect(t).toMatchObject({ mushroom: 3, rock: 4, fern: 7, sedge: 7, log: 9, birch: 9, maple: 11 });
    for (const k of LEAF_KINDS) expect(h.leaf[k]).toBe(1);
    expect(h.trees.count).toBe(5);
    expect(t.trees.count).toBe(2);
  });

  it('True leaves are each species\' real length on average', () => {
    for (const k of LEAF_KINDS) {
      const meanLength = SCALE_SPECS.true.leaf[k] * LEAF_SIZE[k] * 0.9;
      expect(meanLength / UNITS_PER_CM).toBeCloseTo(REAL_LEAF_CM[k], 1);
    }
  });

  it('switches the active spec', () => {
    expect(scaleSpec()).toBe(SCALE_SPECS[DEFAULT_SCALE]);
    expect(setScale('true')).toBe(SCALE_SPECS.true);
    expect(scaleSpec().mode).toBe('true');
  });

  it('knows its mode names', () => {
    expect(isScaleMode('hybrid')).toBe(true);
    expect(isScaleMode('TRUE')).toBe(false);
    expect(isScaleMode(undefined)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('leaf counts follow leaf area', () => {
  it('Classic and Hybrid keep the preset counts', () => {
    expect(leafCountScale(SCALE_SPECS.classic)).toBe(1);
    expect(leafCountScale(SCALE_SPECS.hybrid)).toBe(1);
    expect(scaledLeafCount(1500, SCALE_SPECS.classic)).toBe(1500);
  });
  it('True has about a quarter as many leaves, each about four times the area', () => {
    const f = leafCountScale(SCALE_SPECS.true);
    expect(f).toBeGreaterThan(0.22);
    expect(f).toBeLessThan(0.3);
    expect(scaledLeafCount(1500, SCALE_SPECS.true)).toBe(Math.round(1500 * f));
    expect(scaledLeafCount(0, SCALE_SPECS.true)).toBe(0);
    expect(scaledLeafCount(1, SCALE_SPECS.true)).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

Create `src/surface/leafScale.ts`:

```ts
import { LEAF_KINDS, type LeafKind } from './leafLitter';
import { leafShape } from './leafShapes';
import { LEAF_SIZE } from './leafPalette';
import { scaleSpec, type ScaleSpec } from './worldScale';

/** A species' silhouette area at length 1 (its outline, leaf units). */
function shapeArea(kind: LeafKind): number {
  const s = leafShape(kind);
  let a = 0;
  for (let j = 0; j < s.outline; j++) {
    const k = (j + 1) % s.outline;
    a += s.points[j * 2] * s.points[k * 2 + 1] - s.points[k * 2] * s.points[j * 2 + 1];
  }
  return Math.abs(a) / 2;
}
const AREA = Object.fromEntries(LEAF_KINDS.map((k) => [k, shapeArea(k)])) as Record<LeafKind, number>;

/**
 * Bigger leaves, fewer of them (spec 4.2): a leaf layer's count scales with 1 / leaf area, so the
 * floor stays about as covered (randomly strewn leaves cover a share that depends only on their
 * total area) and overdraw and fill rate stay about the same. Species are drawn equally often, so
 * the area is summed over them, each with its own silhouette. 1 for Classic and Hybrid.
 */
export function leafCountScale(spec: ScaleSpec = scaleSpec()): number {
  let base = 0;
  let scaled = 0;
  for (const k of LEAF_KINDS) {
    base += AREA[k] * LEAF_SIZE[k] ** 2;
    scaled += AREA[k] * (LEAF_SIZE[k] * spec.leaf[k]) ** 2;
  }
  return base / scaled;
}

/** A preset's leaf count (graphics.ts gives Classic's) for the mode: at least 1 when n > 0. */
export function scaledLeafCount(n: number, spec: ScaleSpec = scaleSpec()): number {
  return n > 0 ? Math.max(1, Math.round(n * leafCountScale(spec))) : 0;
}

```

Create `src/surface/worldScale.ts`:

```ts
import type { LeafKind } from './leafLitter';

/**
 * World scale (Step 1b spec section 4): one ScaleSpec per mode, holding every size multiplier
 * and that mode's prop and tree layout. Every size and placement module reads the active spec
 * (scaleSpec()); nothing outside this file knows which mode is on. Picking the final mode after
 * the class vote means changing DEFAULT_SCALE; deleting a mode means deleting its entry.
 *
 * The worm, its speed and the camera are the same in every mode. The worm is 3.5 units long and
 * stands for a 15 cm earthworm, so 1 cm is about 0.23 units. The world stays 40 x 40 units.
 */
export type ScaleMode = 'classic' | 'hybrid' | 'true';
export const SCALE_MODES: readonly ScaleMode[] = ['classic', 'hybrid', 'true'];
/** What a fresh device gets (Matt's pick; the class vote may change it). */
export const DEFAULT_SCALE: ScaleMode = 'classic';

/** World units per real centimetre: the 3.5-unit worm is a 15 cm earthworm. */
export const UNITS_PER_CM = 3.5 / 15;

/** A near tree layout: `count` trunks, seeded, at least `clear` from the spawn. */
export interface TreeLayout { count: number; seed: number; clear: number }
/** A rock at Classic size; it is drawn `rock` times bigger in the mode. */
export interface RockSite { x: number; z: number; size: number }
/** The fallen log at Classic radius and length; `log` scales both. */
export interface LogSite { x: number; z: number; yaw: number; length: number; radius: number }

export interface ScaleSpec {
  readonly mode: ScaleMode;
  /** Size of every leaf (litter, detail, hero) per species, times Classic. */
  readonly leaf: Readonly<Record<LeafKind, number>>;
  /** Clutter sizes, times Classic. */
  readonly mushroom: number;
  readonly fern: number;
  readonly sedge: number;
  /** Rock size, times Classic. */
  readonly rock: number;
  /** The fallen log's radius and length, times Classic. */
  readonly log: number;
  /** Trunk girth and height, times Classic. */
  readonly birch: number;
  readonly maple: number;
  readonly trees: TreeLayout;
  readonly rocks: readonly RockSite[];
  readonly logSite: LogSite;
  /** Ground clutter items at full density, and their seed. */
  readonly clutter: number;
  readonly clutterSeed: number;
  /** The distant tree ring: size times Classic, and how many. */
  readonly distantScale: number;
  readonly distantCount: number;
}

const SAME_LEAVES: Readonly<Record<LeafKind, number>> = { ash: 1, basswood: 1, aspen: 1, maple: 1, oak: 1 };

/**
 * Real leaf lengths (cm) for True: aspen 5.5, a white ash leaflet 8, sugar maple 11, basswood 12,
 * red oak 15. Classic draws a leaf LEAF_SIZE[kind] times 0.6 to 1.2 long (0.9 on average), so each
 * multiplier is the real length in units over that average (tests/unit/worldScale.test.ts).
 */
export const REAL_LEAF_CM: Readonly<Record<LeafKind, number>> = { aspen: 5.5, ash: 8, maple: 11, basswood: 12, oak: 15 };
const TRUE_LEAVES: Readonly<Record<LeafKind, number>> = { aspen: 1.426, ash: 1.975, maple: 1.840, basswood: 1.944, oak: 2.431 };

/** Classic's hand-placed glacial erratics (Step 1a). */
const CLASSIC_ROCKS: readonly RockSite[] = [
  { x: -5.2, z: 7.4, size: 0.9 },
  { x: 7.2, z: -3.6, size: 1.2 },
  { x: -8.2, z: -5.2, size: 0.7 },
  { x: 9.8, z: 8.6, size: 1.0 },
  { x: -11.5, z: 5.5, size: 1.4 },
  { x: 3.2, z: -8.2, size: 0.6 },
  { x: 6.2, z: 2.2, size: 0.45 },
];

/**
 * Hybrid and True: fewer stones, four times bigger (10 to 40 cm), placed around each mode's
 * trees and log, well away from the worm's paths (tests/unit/propPlacement.test.ts).
 */
const HYBRID_ROCKS: readonly RockSite[] = [
  { x: 3.5, z: -11, size: 1.2 },
  { x: -7, z: 11, size: 0.8 },
  { x: 7.5, z: 0, size: 0.7 },
  { x: 13.5, z: -15, size: 0.6 },
  { x: -10, z: 3, size: 0.5 },
];
const TRUE_ROCKS: readonly RockSite[] = [
  { x: -9.5, z: -10, size: 1.2 },
  { x: 7, z: 5.5, size: 0.8 },
  { x: -7.5, z: 7, size: 0.7 },
  { x: 0, z: -15, size: 0.6 },
  { x: 0.5, z: -7, size: 0.5 },
];

export const SCALE_SPECS: Readonly<Record<ScaleMode, ScaleSpec>> = {
  classic: {
    mode: 'classic', leaf: SAME_LEAVES, mushroom: 1, fern: 1, sedge: 1, rock: 1, log: 1, birch: 1, maple: 1,
    trees: { count: 14, seed: 7, clear: 4 },
    rocks: CLASSIC_ROCKS,
    logSite: { x: 3.9, z: 4.7, yaw: -0.5, length: 4.4, radius: 0.32 },
    clutter: 64, clutterSeed: 5, distantScale: 1, distantCount: 72,
  },
  hybrid: {
    mode: 'hybrid', leaf: SAME_LEAVES, mushroom: 3, fern: 7, sedge: 7, rock: 4, log: 4.5, birch: 4.5, maple: 4.5,
    trees: { count: 5, seed: 15, clear: 12.5 },
    rocks: HYBRID_ROCKS,
    logSite: { x: 9, z: 9, yaw: -0.6, length: 4.4, radius: 0.32 },
    clutter: 24, clutterSeed: 5, distantScale: 4.5, distantCount: 24,
  },
  true: {
    mode: 'true', leaf: TRUE_LEAVES, mushroom: 3, fern: 7, sedge: 7, rock: 4, log: 9, birch: 9, maple: 11,
    trees: { count: 2, seed: 284, clear: 18 },
    rocks: TRUE_ROCKS,
    logSite: { x: 0, z: 15.5, yaw: 0, length: 4.4, radius: 0.32 },
    clutter: 24, clutterSeed: 5, distantScale: 9, distantCount: 14,
  },
};

export const SCALE_LABELS: Readonly<Record<ScaleMode, string>> = { classic: 'Classic', hybrid: 'Hybrid', true: 'True' };
export const SCALE_DESCRIPTIONS: Readonly<Record<ScaleMode, string>> = {
  classic: 'The forest as it is now.',
  hybrid: 'Small things real size, big things half.',
  true: 'Everything real size, next to a real worm.',
};

export function isScaleMode(v: unknown): v is ScaleMode {
  return typeof v === 'string' && (SCALE_MODES as readonly string[]).includes(v);
}

let active: ScaleSpec = SCALE_SPECS[DEFAULT_SCALE];

/** The active mode's spec. Modules read it when they build or place things. */
export function scaleSpec(): ScaleSpec {
  return active;
}

/**
 * Makes `mode` active. Everything built from the spec must then be rebuilt (src/game/world.ts);
 * caches keyed on the spec (treeSites, footprints, the height grid) refresh on their next read.
 */
export function setScale(mode: ScaleMode): ScaleSpec {
  active = SCALE_SPECS[mode];
  return active;
}
```

`wormsim.settings` gains `scale` (spec 4.3). It stays version 1: a missing or unknown value reads as the default, so a Step 1a save loads without change (CLAUDE.md, "One origin, shared saves"). Two additions keep every version's saves safe: a write keeps any stored fields this build does not know (a later version's), and `updateSettings(store, patch)` changes only the given fields, so the Graphics menu and the World scale panel never overwrite each other's choice. `GraphicsControl` saves through it. `?scale=classic|hybrid|true` forces a mode for the visit (tests and gates) without saving it; Task 13 wires it up.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { parseParams } from '../../src/game/params';

describe('parseParams', () => {
  it('defaults: no forced preset or scale, no fps tag, not quick, Start screen', () => {
    expect(parseParams('')).toEqual({ preset: null, scale: null, fps: false, quick: false, speedTest: false });
  });
  it('reads ?preset=, ?scale=, ?fps=1, ?quick=1 and ?speedtest=1', () => {
    expect(parseParams('?preset=ultra&scale=hybrid&fps=1&quick=1&speedtest=1'))
      .toEqual({ preset: 'ultra', scale: 'hybrid', fps: true, quick: true, speedTest: true });
  });
  it('ignores an unknown preset (and Auto, which is not a preset)', () => {
    expect(parseParams('?preset=extreme').preset).toBeNull();
    expect(parseParams('?preset=auto').preset).toBeNull();
  });
  it('reads each scale mode and ignores an unknown one', () => {
    expect(parseParams('?scale=classic').scale).toBe('classic');
    expect(parseParams('?scale=true').scale).toBe('true');
    expect(parseParams('?scale=huge').scale).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  loadSettings, saveSettings, updateSettings, DEFAULT_SETTINGS, SETTINGS_KEY, type KeyValueStore,
} from '../../src/save/settingsStore';
import { DEFAULT_SCALE } from '../../src/surface/worldScale';

function memory(initial: Record<string, string> = {}): KeyValueStore & { data: Record<string, string> } {
  const data = { ...initial };
  return {
    data,
    getItem: (k) => (k in data ? data[k] : null),
    setItem: (k, v) => { data[k] = v; },
  };
}

const throwing: KeyValueStore = {
  getItem: () => { throw new Error('SecurityError'); },
  setItem: () => { throw new Error('QuotaExceededError'); },
};

const stored = (raw: object) => memory({ [SETTINGS_KEY]: JSON.stringify(raw) });

describe('settings store', () => {
  it('uses the wormsim.settings key', () => {
    expect(SETTINGS_KEY).toBe('wormsim.settings');
  });

  it('defaults to Auto with nothing settled, at the default world scale', () => {
    expect(DEFAULT_SETTINGS).toEqual({ version: 1, graphics: 'auto', autoSettled: null, scale: DEFAULT_SCALE });
    expect(loadSettings(memory())).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(loadSettings(null)).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  });

  it('round-trips what it saves', () => {
    const store = memory();
    const s = { version: 1, graphics: 'ultra', autoSettled: 'medium', scale: 'hybrid' } as const;
    expect(saveSettings(store, s)).toBe(true);
    expect(JSON.parse(store.data[SETTINGS_KEY])).toEqual(s);
    expect(loadSettings(store)).toEqual(s);
  });

  it('reads a Step 1a save (no scale) as the default scale, keeping its graphics', () => {
    expect(loadSettings(stored({ version: 1, graphics: 'high', autoSettled: 'medium' })))
      .toEqual({ version: 1, graphics: 'high', autoSettled: 'medium', scale: DEFAULT_SCALE });
  });

  it('falls back to defaults on corrupt JSON', () => {
    expect(loadSettings(memory({ [SETTINGS_KEY]: '{not json' }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(loadSettings(memory({ [SETTINGS_KEY]: 'null' }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(loadSettings(memory({ [SETTINGS_KEY]: '[1,2]' }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  });

  it('falls back to defaults on a wrong version', () => {
    expect(loadSettings(stored({ version: 2, graphics: 'low', autoSettled: 'low', scale: 'true' }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  });

  it('replaces unknown field values with their defaults', () => {
    expect(loadSettings(stored({ version: 1, graphics: 'extreme', autoSettled: 'ultra', scale: 'giant' }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(loadSettings(stored({ version: 1, graphics: 'low', autoSettled: 42, scale: 7 })))
      .toEqual({ version: 1, graphics: 'low', autoSettled: null, scale: DEFAULT_SCALE });
  });

  it('keeps fields it does not know when it saves (a later version\'s)', () => {
    const store = stored({ version: 1, graphics: 'low', autoSettled: null, scale: 'true', moisture: { muted: true } });
    saveSettings(store, { ...loadSettings(store), graphics: 'high' });
    expect(JSON.parse(store.data[SETTINGS_KEY])).toEqual({
      version: 1, graphics: 'high', autoSettled: null, scale: 'true', moisture: { muted: true },
    });
  });

  it('updateSettings changes only what it is given', () => {
    const store = stored({ version: 1, graphics: 'low', autoSettled: 'medium', scale: 'classic', later: 1 });
    expect(updateSettings(store, { scale: 'true' })).toEqual({ version: 1, graphics: 'low', autoSettled: 'medium', scale: 'true' });
    expect(updateSettings(store, { graphics: 'ultra' }).scale).toBe('true');
    expect(JSON.parse(store.data[SETTINGS_KEY])).toEqual({
      version: 1, graphics: 'ultra', autoSettled: 'medium', scale: 'true', later: 1,
    });
  });

  it('survives storage that throws, on read and on write', () => {
    expect(loadSettings(throwing)).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(saveSettings(throwing, { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, graphics: 'low' })).toBe(false);
    expect(saveSettings(null, { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, graphics: 'low' })).toBe(false);
    expect(updateSettings(throwing, { scale: 'hybrid' }).scale).toBe('hybrid');
  });
});
```

Replace the whole of `src/save/settingsStore.ts` with:

```ts
import { isChoice, isPresetId, AUTO_ORDER, type GraphicsChoice, type PresetId } from '../render/graphics';
import { DEFAULT_SCALE, isScaleMode, type ScaleMode } from '../surface/worldScale';

/**
 * Player settings, one localStorage key (spec 3.2). Every read and write is guarded: missing,
 * corrupt, wrong-version or unreadable data gives the defaults, and a failed write (private
 * mode, blocked storage, full quota) just means the settings last for this session only.
 * The full save system (sub-project 5) will absorb this key later.
 *
 * Every version of the game runs on one origin and shares this key (CLAUDE.md): a missing or
 * unknown field reads as its default, and a write keeps fields this build does not know (a later
 * version's), so playing an older version never wipes a newer one's settings.
 */
export const SETTINGS_KEY = 'wormsim.settings';

export interface Settings {
  version: 1;
  graphics: GraphicsChoice;
  /** The preset Auto last settled on (never Ultra), or null. */
  autoSettled: PresetId | null;
  /** World scale (Step 1b spec 4.3). Added in V1; older saves have none and read as the default. */
  scale: ScaleMode;
}

export const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: Readonly<Settings> = Object.freeze({
  version: 1, graphics: 'auto', autoSettled: null, scale: DEFAULT_SCALE,
});

/** The two Storage methods we use, so tests can pass a fake. */
export interface KeyValueStore {
  getItem(key: string): string | null;
  setItem(key: string, value: string): void;
}

/** window.localStorage, or null where even touching it throws (sandboxed or blocked). */
export function browserStore(): KeyValueStore | null {
  try {
    return window.localStorage;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** The stored version-1 object as it is (unknown fields included), or null. Throws if storage does. */
function storedObject(store: KeyValueStore): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  const raw = store.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY);
  if (raw === null) return null;
  try {
    const v: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (typeof v !== 'object' || v === null || Array.isArray(v)) return null;
    const o = v as Record<string, unknown>;
    return o.version === 1 ? o : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function loadSettings(store: KeyValueStore | null): Settings {
  const defaults: Settings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
  if (!store) return defaults;
  try {
    const o = storedObject(store);
    if (!o) return defaults;
    return {
      version: 1,
      graphics: isChoice(o.graphics) ? o.graphics : defaults.graphics,
      autoSettled: isPresetId(o.autoSettled) && AUTO_ORDER.includes(o.autoSettled) ? o.autoSettled : null,
      scale: isScaleMode(o.scale) ? o.scale : defaults.scale,
    };
  } catch {
    return defaults;
  }
}

/** Writes `s`, keeping any stored fields this build does not know. False when it could not be stored. */
export function saveSettings(store: KeyValueStore | null, s: Settings): boolean {
  if (!store) return false;
  try {
    const kept = storedObject(store) ?? {};
    store.setItem(SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify({
      ...kept, version: 1, graphics: s.graphics, autoSettled: s.autoSettled, scale: s.scale,
    }));
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Changes some settings and keeps the rest as stored, so two owners (the Graphics menu and the
 * World scale panel) never overwrite each other's choice. Returns the settings as now saved (or as
 * they would be, if storage failed).
 */
export function updateSettings(store: KeyValueStore | null, patch: Partial<Omit<Settings, 'version'>>): Settings {
  const next: Settings = { ...loadSettings(store), ...patch, version: 1 };
  saveSettings(store, next);
  return next;
}
```

### Task 6: Trees, rocks, the log and clutter laid out from the ScaleSpec; solid footprints (rendering)

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/surface/groundProps.ts`
- Modify (whole file): `src/surface/heroObstacles.ts`
- Modify: `src/surface/heroSpawn.ts`
- Modify: `src/surface/litterDepth.ts`
- Create: `src/surface/obstacles.ts`
- Modify (whole file): `src/surface/propPlacement.ts`
- Modify: `src/surface/terrainMesh.ts`
- Modify: `src/surface/treeShadows.ts`
- Modify (whole file): `src/surface/treeSites.ts`
- Create: `src/surface/wormPaths.ts`
- Modify (whole file): `tests/unit/heroObstacles.test.ts`
- Modify: `tests/unit/litterDepth.test.ts`
- Modify (whole file): `tests/unit/propPlacement.test.ts`
- Modify: `tests/unit/surfaceHeight.test.ts`

Every placement module reads the active spec (spec 4.2), through caches keyed on the spec object, so `setScale()` is all it takes: `treeSites()` (seeded per mode), `heroLog()` and `rocks()` (at their drawn size), `placeClutter()` (the mode's count and seed, radii grown with the clutter size, at least three of each kind where they fit), litter drifts, trunk shadows and the terrain's height grid. The speed test's loop and the spawn's straight run move to `src/surface/wormPaths.ts` so tree placement can use them without an import cycle.

`src/surface/obstacles.ts` builds the solid footprints from the same layout (spec 4.4): circles for trunks (their measured root flare; a birch clump's circle holds both stems) and rocks, a capsule for the log. `heroObstacles.ts` keeps only `unwedge`, now on these footprints, and a respawning hero leaf whose every candidate spot lands in a big footprint searches rings round the head for a free spot.

Rocks and the log change size here (Hybrid and True only); trunks, clutter and the distant ring follow in Task 7. Classic stays byte-for-byte Step 1a: the new checksum test pins its trees, clutter and litter depth.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll } from 'vitest';
import { WEDGE_TIME } from '../../src/surface/heroObstacles';
import { obstacleDepth, ROCK_FOOT, LOG_FOOT } from '../../src/surface/obstacles';
import { createHeroLeaves, setHeroCount, stepHeroLeaves, HERO_MAX } from '../../src/surface/heroLeaves';
import { LEAF_REACH } from '../../src/surface/heroClearance';
import { OBSTACLE_PAD } from '../../src/surface/heroSpawn';
import { treeSites, trunkFootOf } from '../../src/surface/treeSites';
import { heroLog, rocks } from '../../src/surface/propPlacement';
import { DEFAULT_SCALE, SCALE_MODES, setScale } from '../../src/surface/worldScale';
import { PLAY_HALF } from '../../src/surface/terrain';

const DT = 1 / 60;
const flat = () => 0.05;
const NONE = new Float32Array(0);
const hit = { nx: 0, nz: 0 };

/** One leaf at (x, z), at rest, with the head nearby (so it is not recycled for distance). */
function oneLeaf(x: number, z: number) {
  const p = createHeroLeaves(1, 3);
  setHeroCount(p, 1, x, z, 0, flat);
  p.x[0] = p.px[0] = x;
  p.z[0] = p.pz[0] = z;
  return p;
}
const pad = (p: ReturnType<typeof oneLeaf>) => OBSTACLE_PAD * p.size[0] * LEAF_REACH[p.kind[0]];

/** A spot in the play area 0.3 outside a footprint of radius `r` round (x, z), clear of the others. */
function besideOf(x: number, z: number, r: number): { x: number; z: number } {
  for (let a = 0; a < 2 * Math.PI; a += Math.PI / 8) {
    const p = { x: x + Math.cos(a) * (r + 0.3), z: z + Math.sin(a) * (r + 0.3) };
    if (Math.abs(p.x) < PLAY_HALF - 0.5 && Math.abs(p.z) < PLAY_HALF - 0.5 && obstacleDepth(p.x, p.z, 0.25, hit) <= 0) return p;
  }
  throw new Error(`no free spot beside (${x}, ${z})`);
}

afterAll(() => { setScale(DEFAULT_SCALE); });

describe.each(SCALE_MODES)('%s: hero leaves and trunks, rocks and the log', (mode) => {
  beforeAll(() => { setScale(mode); });
  const tree = () => treeSites()[0];
  /** Just outside the first trunk's footprint, on its +x side. */
  const beside = (gap: number) => tree().x + trunkFootOf(tree()) + gap;

  it('finds the footprints of trunks, rocks and the log', () => {
    expect(obstacleDepth(tree().x, tree().z, 0, hit)).toBeCloseTo(trunkFootOf(tree()), 6);
    const r = rocks()[0];
    expect(obstacleDepth(r.x, r.z, 0, hit)).toBeCloseTo(r.size * ROCK_FOOT, 6);
    const log = heroLog();
    expect(obstacleDepth(log.x, log.z, 0, hit)).toBeCloseTo(log.radius * LOG_FOOT, 6);
    expect(obstacleDepth(0, 0, 0.1, hit)).toBeLessThan(0);
    // Just outside a trunk, the way out points away from it.
    obstacleDepth(beside(-0.1), tree().z, 0.2, hit);
    expect(hit.nx).toBeCloseTo(1, 6);
  });

  it('a leaf shoved into a trunk ends every step outside its footprint (plus half its reach)', () => {
    const p = oneLeaf(beside(0.8), tree().z + 0.05);
    p.vx[0] = -3;
    const spawned = p.spawned;
    for (let s = 0; s < 40; s++) {
      stepHeroLeaves(p, DT, NONE, 0, p.x[0], p.z[0], 0, flat);
      expect(obstacleDepth(p.x[0], p.z[0], pad(p), hit)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1e-4);
    }
    // It slid in once and stopped against it: not wedged, not recycled.
    expect(p.spawned).toBe(spawned);
    expect(p.wedged[0]).toBe(0);
  });

  it('a leaf held against a trunk is recycled after about half a second', () => {
    const x = beside(0.6);
    const p = oneLeaf(x, tree().z);
    const spawned = p.spawned;
    let steps = 0;
    while (p.spawned === spawned && steps < 120) {
      // Something keeps driving it into the trunk.
      p.vx[0] = -2;
      p.vz[0] = 0;
      stepHeroLeaves(p, DT, NONE, 0, x, tree().z, 0, flat);
      steps++;
    }
    expect(p.spawned).toBe(spawned + 1);
    expect(steps * DT).toBeGreaterThan(WEDGE_TIME);
    expect(steps * DT).toBeLessThan(WEDGE_TIME + 0.4);
    // ...to a spot clear of every obstacle.
    expect(obstacleDepth(p.x[0], p.z[0], pad(p), hit)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
  });

  it('new and recycled leaves land clear of trunks, rocks and the log', () => {
    const log = heroLog();
    const sites = [
      ...treeSites().map((t) => besideOf(t.x, t.z, trunkFootOf(t))),
      ...rocks().map((k) => besideOf(k.x, k.z, k.size * ROCK_FOOT)),
      besideOf(log.x, log.z, log.radius * LOG_FOOT),
    ];
    let checked = 0;
    for (const { x, z } of sites) {
      const p = createHeroLeaves(HERO_MAX, 5);
      setHeroCount(p, HERO_MAX, x, z, 0, flat);
      for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) {
        const pd = OBSTACLE_PAD * p.size[i] * LEAF_REACH[p.kind[i]];
        expect(obstacleDepth(p.x[i], p.z[i], pd, hit)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
      }
      // Every leaf left far behind: each is recycled ahead of the head, which faces the obstacle.
      for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) p.x[i] = x + 10;
      stepHeroLeaves(p, DT, NONE, 0, x, z, -Math.PI / 2, flat);
      for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) {
        const pd = OBSTACLE_PAD * p.size[i] * LEAF_REACH[p.kind[i]];
        expect(obstacleDepth(p.x[i], p.z[i], pd, hit)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
        checked++;
      }
    }
    expect(checked).toBeGreaterThan(80);
  });
});
```

Replace the whole of `tests/unit/propPlacement.test.ts` with:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll } from 'vitest';
import {
  placeClutter, keepsClear, heroLog, rocks, CLUTTER_KINDS, logSegmentDistance, clutterRadius,
} from '../../src/surface/propPlacement';
import { treeSites, trunkFootOf } from '../../src/surface/treeSites';
import { obstacleDepth, footprints, LOG_FOOT, ROCK_FOOT } from '../../src/surface/obstacles';
import { CORRIDOR } from '../../src/surface/wormPaths';
import { stepWorm, type WormMotion } from '../../src/worm/locomotion';
import { PLAY_HALF } from '../../src/surface/terrain';
import { AUTO_DRIVE_MOVE, LOCOMOTION } from '../../src/game/drive';
import { DEFAULT_SCALE, SCALE_MODES, SCALE_SPECS, setScale } from '../../src/surface/worldScale';
import { litterDepth } from '../../src/surface/litterDepth';

/** The speed test's auto-drive: the exact move and locomotion options the game uses. */
function autoDrivePath(seconds: number): { x: number; z: number }[] {
  let m: WormMotion = { head: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, heading: 0 };
  const out = [];
  const dt = 1 / 60;
  for (let t = 0; t < seconds; t += dt) {
    m = stepWorm(m, AUTO_DRIVE_MOVE.move, dt, LOCOMOTION);
    out.push({ x: m.head.x, z: m.head.z });
  }
  return out;
}

const path = autoDrivePath(30);
/** Worm half-width plus slack, and the trailing camera (2.6 back, 1.1 up). */
const PATH_CLEARANCE = 1.0;
const hit = { nx: 0, nz: 0 };

function minDistToPath(x: number, z: number): number {
  return Math.min(...path.map((p) => Math.hypot(p.x - x, p.z - z)));
}

afterAll(() => { setScale(DEFAULT_SCALE); });

describe.each(SCALE_MODES)('%s layout', (mode) => {
  beforeAll(() => { setScale(mode); });

  it('keepsClear rejects the spawn point, the auto-drive loop and tree trunks', () => {
    expect(keepsClear(0, 0, 0.1)).toBe(false);
    expect(keepsClear(0, 5, 0.1)).toBe(false);
    for (const p of path.filter((_, i) => i % 30 === 0)) expect(keepsClear(p.x, p.z, 0.1)).toBe(false);
    for (const t of treeSites()) expect(keepsClear(t.x, t.z, 0.1)).toBe(false);
    expect(keepsClear(PLAY_HALF + 0.5, 0, 0.1)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('keeps every solid footprint off the speed test\'s loop, with room for the body and camera', () => {
    for (const p of path) expect(obstacleDepth(p.x, p.z, PATH_CLEARANCE, hit)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
  });

  it('keeps the spawn and the camera behind it clear', () => {
    expect(obstacleDepth(0, 0, 3, hit)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
    expect(obstacleDepth(0, -2.6, 1, hit)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
  });

  it('keeps the fallen log clear of the worm paths and the trunks', () => {
    const log = heroLog();
    for (const p of path) expect(logSegmentDistance(p.x, p.z)).toBeGreaterThan(log.radius * LOG_FOOT + PATH_CLEARANCE + 1);
    // Straight-ahead spawn corridor (+z) stays open.
    for (let z = -2; z <= 7; z += 0.5) expect(logSegmentDistance(0, z)).toBeGreaterThan(log.radius * LOG_FOOT + 1.2);
    for (const t of treeSites()) expect(logSegmentDistance(t.x, t.z)).toBeGreaterThan(log.radius * LOG_FOOT + trunkFootOf(t));
  });

  it('places rocks clear of everything and of each other', () => {
    const log = heroLog();
    const rs = rocks();
    for (const r of rs) {
      const foot = r.size * ROCK_FOOT;
      expect(keepsClear(r.x, r.z, foot)).toBe(true);
      expect(minDistToPath(r.x, r.z)).toBeGreaterThan(foot + PATH_CLEARANCE);
      expect(logSegmentDistance(r.x, r.z)).toBeGreaterThan(foot + log.radius * LOG_FOOT);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < rs.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < rs.length; j++) {
        expect(Math.hypot(rs[i].x - rs[j].x, rs[i].z - rs[j].z)).toBeGreaterThan((rs[i].size + rs[j].size) * ROCK_FOOT);
      }
    }
  });

  it('places the mode\'s clutter clear of the worm paths, trunks, the log and rocks', () => {
    const items = placeClutter();
    expect(items).toHaveLength(SCALE_SPECS[mode].clutter);
    const log = heroLog();
    for (const c of items) {
      expect(CLUTTER_KINDS).toContain(c.kind);
      expect(Number.isFinite(c.x) && Number.isFinite(c.z) && Number.isFinite(c.rot)).toBe(true);
      if (items.indexOf(c) < 4) continue; // the mushrooms by the log, checked below
      const r = clutterRadius(c.kind, c.scale);
      expect(keepsClear(c.x, c.z, r)).toBe(true);
      expect(minDistToPath(c.x, c.z)).toBeGreaterThan(PATH_CLEARANCE);
      expect(logSegmentDistance(c.x, c.z)).toBeGreaterThan(log.radius);
      for (const k of rocks()) expect(Math.hypot(c.x - k.x, c.z - k.z)).toBeGreaterThan(k.size);
    }
    // Mushrooms grow right beside the log.
    expect(items.slice(0, 4).every((c) => c.kind === 'mushrooms'
      && logSegmentDistance(c.x, c.z) > log.radius && logSegmentDistance(c.x, c.z) < log.radius + 0.6 * SCALE_SPECS[mode].mushroom)).toBe(true);
    for (const k of CLUTTER_KINDS) expect(items.some((c) => c.kind === k)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('has as many footprints as trunks and rocks', () => {
    expect(footprints().round.length).toBe((treeSites().length + rocks().length) * 4);
  });
});

describe('the bigger modes keep the spawn\'s straight run clear of trunks', () => {
  it.each(['hybrid', 'true'] as const)('%s', (mode) => {
    setScale(mode);
    for (const t of treeSites()) {
      const nx = Math.max(CORRIDOR.x0, Math.min(CORRIDOR.x1, t.x));
      const nz = Math.max(CORRIDOR.z0, Math.min(CORRIDOR.z1, t.z));
      expect(Math.hypot(t.x - nx, t.z - nz)).toBeGreaterThan(trunkFootOf(t) + PATH_CLEARANCE);
    }
  });
});

describe('placeClutter', () => {
  it('is deterministic per seed', () => {
    setScale('classic');
    const items = placeClutter(60, 5);
    expect(items).toHaveLength(60);
    expect(placeClutter(60, 5)).toEqual(items);
    expect(placeClutter(60, 6)).not.toEqual(items);
  });
});

describe('Classic is exactly the Step 1a world (checksums taken before the scale modes)', () => {
  it('trees, clutter and litter depth', () => {
    setScale('classic');
    const t = treeSites();
    expect(t).toHaveLength(14);
    expect(t.reduce((s, c) => s + c.x, 0)).toBeCloseTo(-70.84152821917087, 10);
    expect(t.reduce((s, c) => s + c.z, 0)).toBeCloseTo(9.825550918467343, 10);
    const c = placeClutter(64);
    expect(c.reduce((s, i) => s + i.x, 0)).toBeCloseTo(-35.457261321132044, 10);
    expect(c.reduce((s, i) => s + i.z, 0)).toBeCloseTo(-23.469567911623017, 10);
    expect(c.reduce((s, i) => s + i.scale, 0)).toBeCloseTo(65.08443361632527, 10);
    let depth = 0;
    let n = 0;
    for (let x = -20; x <= 20; x += 0.37) for (let z = -20; z <= 20; z += 0.41) { depth += litterDepth(x, z); n++; }
    expect(n).toBe(10682);
    expect(depth).toBeCloseTo(425.16354611935066, 8);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { obstacleDepth, type ObstacleHit } from './obstacles';
import type { HeroLeaves } from './heroPool';

/**
 * Hero leaves and the solid props (pure): a leaf whose centre comes within `pad` of a trunk, rock
 * or the log (their footprints, obstacles.ts) is pushed back out, so it never wedges up a trunk.
 */

/** Pushed back out of an obstacle this long (s): the leaf is recycled. */
export const WEDGE_TIME = 0.5;
/** Deeper than this (m) in a footprint counts as in it (not a rounding hair). */
const WEDGE_EPS = 1e-4;
const hit: ObstacleHit = { nx: 0, nz: 0 };

/**
 * Leaf i, its centre within `pad` of a trunk, rock or the log, goes straight back out, losing
 * the part of its velocity into it. Returns true once it has been wedged there for longer than
 * WEDGE_TIME (it would only be propped up against it: recycle it).
 */
export function unwedge(p: HeroLeaves, i: number, pad: number, dt: number): boolean {
  const into = obstacleDepth(p.x[i], p.z[i], pad, hit);
  if (!(into > WEDGE_EPS)) {
    p.wedged[i] = 0;
    return false;
  }
  p.x[i] += hit.nx * into;
  p.z[i] += hit.nz * into;
  const vn = p.vx[i] * hit.nx + p.vz[i] * hit.nz;
  if (vn < 0) {
    p.vx[i] -= vn * hit.nx;
    p.vz[i] -= vn * hit.nz;
  }
  p.wedged[i] += dt;
  return p.wedged[i] > WEDGE_TIME;
}
```

```ts
export const OBSTACLE_PAD = 0.5;
const hit: ObstacleHit = { nx: 0, nz: 0 };

/** The fallback search: RINGS rings of RING_SPOTS spots, RING_STEP (m) apart, round the head. */
const RINGS = 6;
const RING_SPOTS = 12;
const RING_STEP = 0.7;

/**
 * Whether a leaf reaching `clear0` from its centre at (x, z) is off every trunk, rock and the log
 * (by OBSTACLE_PAD of its reach) and off the body (`count` pushers, by its reach plus BODY_GAP).
 */
function freeSpot(x: number, z: number, clear0: number, pushers: Float32Array, count: number): boolean {
  if (obstacleDepth(x, z, OBSTACLE_PAD * clear0, hit) > 0) return false;
  const clear = clear0 + BODY_GAP;
  for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) {
    const o = k * PUSHER_STRIDE;
    const dx = pushers[o] - x;
    const dz = pushers[o + 1] - z;
    const lim = pushers[o + 2] + clear;
    if (dx * dx + dz * dz < lim * lim) return false;
  }
  return true;
}
```

```ts
/** The deepest drift at (x, z) from trunks, rocks and the log. */
export function driftDepth(x: number, z: number): number {
  let best = 0;
  for (const o of ROUND) {
    const dx = x - o.x;
    const dz = z - o.z;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    if (d > o.r + 1.2) continue;
    best = Math.max(best, drift(d - o.r, dx, dz, d, o.r, o.peak));

  const t = Math.max(-HERO_LOG.length / 2, Math.min(HERO_LOG.length / 2,
    (x - HERO_LOG.x) * LOG_DX + (z - HERO_LOG.z) * LOG_DZ));
  const lx = x - (HERO_LOG.x + LOG_DX * t);
  const lz = z - (HERO_LOG.z + LOG_DZ * t);
  const ld = Math.hypot(lx, lz);
  if (ld < HERO_LOG.radius + 1.2) {
    best = Math.max(best, drift(ld - HERO_LOG.radius, lx, lz, ld, HERO_LOG.radius, 0.2));
  }
  return best;
}
```

```ts

/** The deepest drift at (x, z) from trunks, rocks and the log. */
export function driftDepth(x: number, z: number): number {
  const { round, log, logDx: LOG_DX, logDz: LOG_DZ } = driftSources();
  let best = 0;
  for (const o of round) {
    const dx = x - o.x;
    const dz = z - o.z;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    if (d > o.r + 1.2) continue;
    best = Math.max(best, drift(d - o.r, dx, dz, d, o.r, o.peak));
  }
  const t = Math.max(-log.length / 2, Math.min(log.length / 2, (x - log.x) * LOG_DX + (z - log.z) * LOG_DZ));
  const lx = x - (log.x + LOG_DX * t);
  const lz = z - (log.z + LOG_DZ * t);
  const ld = Math.hypot(lx, lz);
  if (ld < log.radius + 1.2) {
    best = Math.max(best, drift(ld - log.radius, lx, lz, ld, log.radius, 0.2));
  }
  return best;
}
```

Create `src/surface/obstacles.ts`:

```ts
import { treeSites, trunkFootOf } from './treeSites';
import { heroLog, rocks } from './propPlacement';
import { scaleSpec, type ScaleSpec } from './worldScale';

/**
 * The solid things on the forest floor, as footprints on the ground (pure; Step 1b spec 4.4):
 * circles for the trunks (their root flare) and rocks, a capsule for the fallen log. Built from
 * the active ScaleSpec. The worm slides along them (crawl.ts), hero leaves are pushed back out of
 * them (heroObstacles.ts), and the camera stops short of the tall ones (followCamera.ts).
 */

/** A drawn rock reaches about this much past its size at ground level (its lumpy, wide flank). */
export const ROCK_FOOT = 1.2;
/** The drawn log is this much wider than its radius (bark lumps), at the height of the worm... */
export const LOG_FOOT = 1.15;
/** ...and its jagged broken ends reach this far (m) past its length (writeLog). */
export const LOG_END = 0.18;
/** A rock's top above the ground, per unit of its size; the log's, per unit of its radius. */
const ROCK_TOP = 0.6;
const LOG_TOP = 1.7;

export interface Footprints {
  /** Round footprints, [x, z, radius, top] each: trunks (top Infinity), then rocks. */
  readonly round: Float64Array;
  /** The log: centre, unit axis, half length, radius and top. */
  readonly log: { x: number; z: number; dx: number; dz: number; half: number; r: number; top: number };
}

let cache: { spec: ScaleSpec; fp: Footprints } | null = null;

/** The active mode's footprints (built once per mode). Read-only. */
export function footprints(): Footprints {
  const spec = scaleSpec();
  if (cache?.spec === spec) return cache.fp;
  const log = heroLog(spec);
  const fp: Footprints = {
    round: Float64Array.from([
      ...treeSites().flatMap((t) => [t.x, t.z, trunkFootOf(t, spec), Infinity]),
      ...rocks().flatMap((k) => [k.x, k.z, k.size * ROCK_FOOT, k.size * ROCK_TOP]),
    ]),
    log: {
      x: log.x, z: log.z, dx: Math.cos(log.yaw), dz: Math.sin(log.yaw), half: log.length / 2 + LOG_END,
      r: log.radius * LOG_FOOT, top: log.radius * LOG_TOP,
    },
  };
  cache = { spec, fp };
  return fp;
}

/** The way out of the deepest obstacle (unit ground vector), written by obstacleDepth. */
export interface ObstacleHit { nx: number; nz: number }

/**
 * How far (m) the point (x, z) lies inside the deepest footprint grown by `pad` (0 or less: clear
 * of them all), and in `out` the direction straight out of it. Allocates nothing.
 */
export function obstacleDepth(x: number, z: number, pad: number, out: ObstacleHit): number {
  const { round, log } = footprints();
  let best = -Infinity;
  for (let o = 0; o < round.length; o += 4) {
    const dx = x - round[o];
    const dz = z - round[o + 1];
    const lim = round[o + 2] + pad;
    if (Math.abs(dx) >= lim || Math.abs(dz) >= lim) continue;
    const d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
    if (lim - d > best) {
      best = lim - d;
      if (d > 1e-9) {
        out.nx = dx / d;
        out.nz = dz / d;
      } else {
        out.nx = 1;
        out.nz = 0;
      }
    }
  }
  // The log: a capsule along its axis.
  const t = Math.max(-log.half, Math.min(log.half, (x - log.x) * log.dx + (z - log.z) * log.dz));
  const dx = x - (log.x + log.dx * t);
  const dz = z - (log.z + log.dz * t);
  const d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
  const into = log.r + pad - d;
  if (into > best) {
    best = into;
    if (d > 1e-9) {
      out.nx = dx / d;
      out.nz = dz / d;
    } else {
      out.nx = -log.dz;
      out.nz = log.dx;
    }
  }
  return best;
}

```

Replace the whole of `src/surface/propPlacement.ts` with:

```ts
import { mulberry32 } from '../core/random';
import { PLAY_HALF } from './terrain';
import { treeSites, trunkFootOf } from './treeSites';
import { clearOfPaths } from './wormPaths';
import { scaleSpec, type LogSite, type RockSite, type ScaleSpec } from './worldScale';

/**
 * Where the forest-floor props go, in the active scale mode (worldScale.ts). Pure apart from
 * reading the active spec. Everything stays out of the worm's way: the straight run ahead of the
 * spawn and the speed test's auto-drive loop (wormPaths.ts), the trailing camera, and the trunks.
 */
const TRUNK_CLEAR = 1.0;

/** True when a prop of `radius` at (x, z) is clear of the worm paths, trunks and world edge. */
export function keepsClear(x: number, z: number, radius: number): boolean {
  if (!clearOfPaths(x, z, radius)) return false;
  if (Math.abs(x) > PLAY_HALF - radius || Math.abs(z) > PLAY_HALF - radius) return false;
  // Classic's trunks keep TRUNK_CLEAR; a bigger trunk keeps its whole footprint.
  return treeSites().every((t) => Math.hypot(x - t.x, z - t.z) > Math.max(TRUNK_CLEAR, trunkFootOf(t)) + radius);
}

/** The hero prop, a mossy fallen paper birch, in `spec`'s mode: its radius and length scaled. */
export function heroLog(spec: ScaleSpec = scaleSpec()): LogSite {
  const s = spec.logSite;
  return { x: s.x, z: s.z, yaw: s.yaw, length: s.length * spec.log, radius: s.radius * spec.log };
}

/** Distance from (x, z) to the log's centre line. */
export function logSegmentDistance(x: number, z: number): number {
  const log = heroLog();
  const dx = Math.cos(log.yaw);
  const dz = Math.sin(log.yaw);
  const t = Math.max(-log.length / 2, Math.min(log.length / 2, (x - log.x) * dx + (z - log.z) * dz));
  return Math.hypot(x - (log.x + dx * t), z - (log.z + dz * t));
}

let rockCache: { spec: ScaleSpec; rocks: readonly RockSite[] } | null = null;

/** Lichen-spotted granite boulders (glacial erratics) in the active mode, at their drawn size. */
export function rocks(): readonly RockSite[] {
  const spec = scaleSpec();
  if (rockCache?.spec !== spec) {
    rockCache = { spec, rocks: spec.rocks.map((r) => ({ x: r.x, z: r.z, size: r.size * spec.rock })) };
  }
  return rockCache.rocks;
}

export type ClutterKind = 'mushrooms' | 'sedge' | 'fern';
export const CLUTTER_KINDS: readonly ClutterKind[] = ['mushrooms', 'sedge', 'fern'];

export interface ClutterItem { x: number; z: number; rot: number; scale: number; kind: ClutterKind }

/** How much bigger than Classic a clutter kind is drawn in `spec`'s mode. */
export function clutterGrow(kind: ClutterKind, spec: ScaleSpec = scaleSpec()): number {
  return kind === 'mushrooms' ? spec.mushroom : kind === 'fern' ? spec.fern : spec.sedge;
}

/** How far a clutter item of `kind` and `scale` reaches from its centre, as drawn in `spec`'s mode. */
export function clutterRadius(kind: ClutterKind, scale: number, spec: ScaleSpec = scaleSpec()): number {
  return (kind === 'fern' ? 0.6 : 0.3) * scale * clutterGrow(kind, spec);
}

function freeOfProps(x: number, z: number, r: number): boolean {
  return logSegmentDistance(x, z) > heroLog().radius + r && rocks().every((k) => Math.hypot(x - k.x, z - k.z) > k.size + r);
}

/** Every kind of clutter gets at least this many items, where they fit. */
const MIN_EACH = 3;

/**
 * Ground clutter: mushroom clusters, sedge tufts and fern clumps (the mode's count and seed by
 * default). The first four are mushrooms growing beside the log; the rest are scattered in
 * random order, so any prefix (the density tiers draw a prefix) is an even thinning. In the big
 * modes a fern needs so much room that random spots rarely fit one, so a kind with fewer than
 * MIN_EACH items then takes over the last items of the commonest kind (Classic never needs this).
 */
export function placeClutter(count = scaleSpec().clutter, seed = scaleSpec().clutterSeed): ClutterItem[] {
  const spec = scaleSpec();
  const log = heroLog(spec);
  const rng = mulberry32(seed);
  const out: ClutterItem[] = [];
  const dx = Math.cos(log.yaw);
  const dz = Math.sin(log.yaw);
  // Along the log's middle stretch: a log longer than Classic's runs off into the distance.
  const stretch = Math.min(log.length, 8);
  for (let k = 0; k < Math.min(4, count); k++) {
    const along = (k / 3 - 0.5) * stretch * 0.7 + (rng() - 0.5) * 0.3;
    const side = k % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const off = log.radius + (0.12 + rng() * 0.1) * spec.mushroom;
    out.push({
      x: log.x + dx * along - dz * off * side,
      z: log.z + dz * along + dx * off * side,
      rot: rng() * Math.PI * 2, scale: 0.8 + rng() * 0.4, kind: 'mushrooms',
    });
  }
  let guard = 0;
  while (out.length < count && guard++ < count * 200) {
    const x = (rng() * 2 - 1) * PLAY_HALF;
    const z = (rng() * 2 - 1) * PLAY_HALF;
    const pick = rng();
    const kind: ClutterKind = pick < 0.3 ? 'mushrooms' : pick < 0.72 ? 'sedge' : 'fern';
    const rot = rng() * Math.PI * 2;
    const scale = 0.7 + rng() * 0.6;
    const r = clutterRadius(kind, scale, spec);
    if (keepsClear(x, z, r) && freeOfProps(x, z, r)) out.push({ x, z, rot, scale, kind });
  }
  for (const kind of CLUTTER_KINDS) {
    for (let tries = 0; tries < 4000 && out.filter((c) => c.kind === kind).length < MIN_EACH; tries++) {
      const x = (rng() * 2 - 1) * PLAY_HALF;
      const z = (rng() * 2 - 1) * PLAY_HALF;
      const rot = rng() * Math.PI * 2;
      const scale = 0.7 + rng() * 0.6;
      const r = clutterRadius(kind, scale, spec);
      if (!keepsClear(x, z, r) || !freeOfProps(x, z, r)) continue;
      if (out.some((c) => c.kind === kind && Math.hypot(c.x - x, c.z - z) < r + clutterRadius(kind, c.scale, spec))) continue;
      const most = CLUTTER_KINDS.reduce((a, b) => (out.filter((c) => c.kind === b).length > out.filter((c) => c.kind === a).length ? b : a));
      let last = out.length - 1;
      while (last >= 4 && out[last].kind !== most) last--;
      if (last < 4) break;
      out[last] = { x, z, rot, scale, kind };
    }
  }
  return out;
}
```

```ts
/**

 * scale mode (the litter drifts against the mode's props). Read-only.
```
