- [ ] **Step 1: Write the doc**

Create `docs/codev/versions/v01-true-size.md`:

````markdown
# V1 · True Size

- **Live:** (set on release day, a Monday). **Form closes:** (that Thursday night).
- **Build:** https://wormsimulator.com/v1/ (commit: set on release)
- **Topic chosen by:** Matt

## Topic
A real earthworm is only about as long as a pencil, so to a worm the forest floor is huge, and
Minnesota's forests grew for about 11,000 years with no earthworms at all.

| Fact shown to students | Source |
|---|---|
| A nightcrawler (the worm in the game) is about 5 to 8 inches (13 to 20 cm) long as an adult. The game's worm is 15 cm. | UMN Extension: Earthworms |
| Minnesota has no native earthworms. | UMN Extension: Earthworms; Minnesota DNR: Earthworms |
| For about 11,000 years after the glaciers melted, Minnesota's forests grew without earthworms. | Minnesota DNR: Earthworms |
| The first earthworms probably came with soil and plants brought from Europe. Fishing bait spreads them now. | Minnesota DNR: Earthworms |
| On their own, earthworms spread less than half a mile in 100 years. | Minnesota DNR: Earthworms |
| About 15 kinds of invasive earthworms live in Minnesota. | USFWS: Minnesota's Can of (Invasive) Worms |

**While you play, notice…** Try all three World scales. In True, how many worm-lengths long is
one big leaf? Which scale feels most like being a real worm, and which is most fun?

## What's new in this build
This matches the "V1 · True Size" entry in `CHANGELOG.md`.
- World scale on the Start screen: Classic, Hybrid or True. True is the default.
- In True, leaves, mushrooms, rocks, ferns, the fallen log and the trees are real size next to a
  15 cm earthworm.
- Trees, rocks and the log are solid: the worm slides along them.
- The camera moves in when a tree or rock is in the way.
- Fixed: leaves no longer shake when the worm crawls under them.
- How to switch: Start screen → **World scale** → pick one → **Back**. Your choice is saved on
  this computer.

## Ballot
### Design vote (Q5): How big should the forest be next to your worm?
| Option (as students read it) | What we'd build | In the build as a toggle? |
|---|---|---|
| Classic: the forest as it was before (small leaves, small logs) | Classic becomes the default for everyone | Yes |
| Hybrid: small things real size, big things half size | Hybrid becomes the default | Yes |
| True: everything real size, next to a real worm | True stays the default | Yes |

Applying the vote is a one-line change (`DEFAULT_SCALE` in `src/surface/worldScale.ts`). The
other modes are deleted once the decision is settled, unless students vote to keep the choice.
A close call (top two within 10 points) ships both as a toggle and goes to a runoff on V2's form.

### Next topic (Q7)
Not on V1's form: Matt picks V1's and V2's topics. The first student topic vote is on V2's form.

### Ideas from last version (Q8)
Not on V1's form: there was no earlier form to collect ideas from.

## Paste-ready form
Title: **Worm Simulator V1 · True Size: Feedback**

1. **Were you able to play V1?** (multiple choice) Yes / It wouldn't load / I didn't have time
2. **About how long did you play?** (multiple choice) Under 5 minutes / 5 to 15 minutes /
   15 to 30 minutes / More than 30 minutes
3. **How long have Minnesota's forests grown without earthworms?** (multiple choice, one correct)
   About 110 years / About 1,100 years / About 11,000 years (correct) / They always had earthworms
4. **How well did the game show how big a worm's world is?** (linear scale 1 to 4: 1 = not at
   all, 4 = really well)
5. **How big should the forest be next to your worm?** (multiple choice) Classic: the forest as
   it was before / Hybrid: small things real size, big things half size / True: everything real
   size / Other: ____
6. **Why did you choose that?** (short answer) Starter: "In the game I noticed ___, and real
   worms ___."
7. *(Not on V1's form: next topic. Starts on V2's form.)*
8. *(Not on V1's form: ideas from last version.)*
9. **Was anything confusing or broken?** (checkboxes) It was slow or choppy / I couldn't find
   World scale / The camera got stuck or jumped / My worm got stuck / Leaves looked strange /
   Nothing / Other: ____
10. **How fun was this version?** (linear scale 1 to 5) and **Your idea** (paragraph, optional):
    "What should we add or change?"

## Slides draft
1. **You said, we did:** "This is V1, the first version you help design. Your votes on this form
   change V2. Next week's first slide shows what you picked."
2. **Topic, part 1: How big is a worm?** "A nightcrawler is about 5 to 8 inches long, about as
   long as a pencil. The worm in our game is 15 cm. To a worm, one oak leaf is a blanket and a
   fallen log is a wall."
   - Image idea: the `true.jpg` screenshot beside a photo of a nightcrawler on a ruler (a photo
     Matt takes or has rights to).
   - Alt text: "The game in True size: the worm under big fall leaves, with a fallen birch log

   - Source footer: UMN Extension, Earthworms.
3. **Topic, part 2: A forest without worms.** "When the glaciers melted about 11,000 years ago,

   mile in 100 years. Now about 15 kinds of invasive earthworms live here."

   - Alt text for each: "The same spot in Classic / Hybrid / True scale."
````
